M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{565795}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\textstyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{439166}{204801}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptstyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{365185}{177495}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptscriptstyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits})}_{\chi}.

We have to study the group
(\mathchoice{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{565795}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\displaystyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{565795}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\textstyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{439166}{204801}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptstyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{365185}{177495}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptscriptstyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{{\underline{E}}}(\mathchoice{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{565795}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\displaystyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{565795}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\textstyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{439166}{204801}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptstyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{365185}{177495}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptscriptstyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}))_{\chi}
. We know that
(\mathchoice{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{565795}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\displaystyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{565795}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\textstyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{439166}{204801}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptstyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{365185}{177495}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptscriptstyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{{\underline{E}}}(\mathchoice{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{565795}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\displaystyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{565795}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\textstyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{439166}{204801}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptstyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{365185}{177495}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptscriptstyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}))/M
is isomorphic to
\operatorname{Z}({{\mathbf{G}}})\rtimes{{\underline{E}}}(\mathchoice{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{565795}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\displaystyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{565795}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\textstyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{439166}{204801}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptstyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{365185}{177495}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptscriptstyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits})
. Let
U
be the subgroup of
\operatorname{Z}({{\mathbf{G}}})\rtimes{{\underline{E}}}(\mathchoice{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{565795}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\displaystyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{565795}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\textstyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{439166}{204801}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptstyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{365185}{177495}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptscriptstyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits})
corresponding to
(\mathchoice{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{565795}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\displaystyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{565795}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\textstyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{439166}{204801}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptstyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{365185}{177495}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptscriptstyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\underline{E}}(\mathchoice{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{565795}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\displaystyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{565795}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\textstyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{439166}{204801}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptstyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{365185}{177495}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptscriptstyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}))_{\chi}/M
.
To get our claim it suffices to ensure
U=(U\cap\operatorname{Z}({{\mathbf{G}}}))\rtimes(U\cap{\underline{E}}(\mathchoice{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{565795}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\displaystyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{565795}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\textstyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{439166}{204801}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptstyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{365185}{177495}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptscriptstyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}))
by showing

\displaystyle\left\langle h_{0}\right\rangle\leq U\leq\left\langle h_{0}\right\rangle\rtimes{\underline{E}}(\mathchoice{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{565795}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\displaystyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{565795}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\textstyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{439166}{204801}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptstyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{365185}{177495}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptscriptstyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}).

The assumption
\chi^{\widehat{t}_{1}}=\chi
with
\mathcal{L}_{{{\nu F_{q}}}}(\widehat{t}_{1})=h_{0}
already implies the inclusion

\left\langle h_{0}\right\rangle\leq U.

Next, we check
U\leq\left\langle h_{0}\right\rangle\rtimes{\underline{E}}(\mathchoice{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{565795}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\displaystyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{565795}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\textstyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{439166}{204801}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptstyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{365185}{177495}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptscriptstyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits})
. If
\phi_{1}\in\mathrm{Irr}(G_{1})
and
\phi_{2}\in\mathrm{Irr}(G_{2})
with
\phi={\phi_{1}.\phi_{2}}
, then
({\mathchoice{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{412222}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\displaystyle G\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{412222}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\textstyle G\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{326422}{204801}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptstyle G\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{278933}{177495}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptscriptstyle G\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}}_{i})_{\phi_{i}}\leq G_{i}\left\langle\widehat{t}_{i}\right\rangle
for some
i\in\{1,2\}
because of
{\mathchoice{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{379200}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\displaystyle T\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{379200}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\textstyle T\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{304069}{204801}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptstyle T\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{263696}{177495}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptscriptstyle T\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}}_{\phi}\leq T\left\langle\widehat{t}_{1}\right\rangle
, where
T={\mathbf{T}}\cap M^{\circ}
. Without loss of generality, we can assume
({\mathchoice{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{412222}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\displaystyle G\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{412222}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\textstyle G\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{326422}{204801}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptstyle G\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{278933}{177495}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptscriptstyle G\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}}_{1})_{\phi}\leq G_{1}\left\langle\widehat{t}_{1}\right\rangle
. According to
Lemma

3.18
, this implies

({\mathchoice{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{412222}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\displaystyle G\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{412222}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\textstyle G\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{326422}{204801}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptstyle G\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{278933}{177495}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptscriptstyle G\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}}_{1}\rtimes{\underline{E}}({\mathchoice{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{412222}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\displaystyle G\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{412222}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\textstyle G\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{326422}{204801}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptstyle G\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{278933}{177495}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptscriptstyle G\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}}_{1}))_{\chi}\leq(G_{1}\left\langle\widehat{t}_{1}\right\rangle)\rtimes{\underline{E}}({\mathchoice{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{412222}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\displaystyle G\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{412222}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\textstyle G\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{326422}{204801}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptstyle G\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{278933}{177495}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptscriptstyle G\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}}_{1}).

Recall from
Definition

5.10
that
{\underline{E}}(\mathchoice{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{565795}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\displaystyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{565795}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\textstyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{439166}{204801}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptstyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{365185}{177495}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptscriptstyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits})
also defines a group of automorphisms of
{{{\mathbf{G}}}^{{{\nu F_{q}}}}}\mathchoice{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{379200}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\displaystyle T\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{379200}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\textstyle T\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{304069}{204801}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptstyle T\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{263696}{177495}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptscriptstyle T\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}
and
{\underline{E}}({\mathchoice{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{565795}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\displaystyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{565795}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\textstyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{439166}{204801}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptstyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{365185}{177495}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptscriptstyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}}^{\circ}):={\underline{E}}(\mathchoice{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{565795}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\displaystyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{565795}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\textstyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{439166}{204801}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptstyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{365185}{177495}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptscriptstyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits})\left\langle n,h_{0}\right\rangle
stabilizes
{\mathchoice{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{565795}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\displaystyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{565795}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\textstyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{439166}{204801}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptstyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{365185}{177495}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptscriptstyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}}^{\circ}=\mathchoice{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{379200}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\displaystyle T\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{379200}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\textstyle T\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{304069}{204801}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptstyle T\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{263696}{177495}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptscriptstyle T\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}M^{\circ}
. Then
\phi
satisfies

(M_{0}\mathchoice{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{379200}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\displaystyle T\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{379200}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\textstyle T\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{304069}{204801}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptstyle T\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{263696}{177495}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptscriptstyle T\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\underline{E}}({\mathchoice{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{565795}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\displaystyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{565795}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\textstyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{439166}{204801}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptstyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{365185}{177495}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptscriptstyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}}^{\circ}))_{\phi}\leq M_{0}\left\langle\widehat{t}_{1},\widehat{t}_{2}\right\rangle{\underline{E}}({\mathchoice{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{565795}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\displaystyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{565795}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\textstyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{439166}{204801}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptstyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{365185}{177495}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptscriptstyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}}^{\circ}).

Since
\chi\in\mathrm{Irr}(M\mid\phi)
, this shows

M(\mathchoice{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{379200}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\displaystyle (\mathchoice{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{565795}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\displaystyle , so we get (

and accordingly

\displaystyle(\mathchoice{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{565795}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\displaystyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{565795}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\textstyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{439166}{204801}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptstyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{365185}{177495}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptscriptstyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\underline{E}}(\mathchoice{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{565795}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\displaystyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{565795}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\textstyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{439166}{204801}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptstyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{365185}{177495}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptscriptstyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}))_{\chi}

\displaystyle={\mathchoice{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{565795}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\displaystyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{565795}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\textstyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{439166}{204801}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptstyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{365185}{177495}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptscriptstyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}}_{\chi}{{\underline{E}}(\mathchoice{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{565795}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\displaystyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{565795}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\textstyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{439166}{204801}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptstyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{365185}{177495}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptscriptstyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits})}_{\chi}.\qed

For the proof of
Theorem

5.20
, we finish describing the characters of
M
via the ones of
M_{0}
. Notice that
{\underline{E}}(M^{\circ})
stabilizes
G_{1}
and
G_{2}
.

Proposition
\@upn
5.23

Let
\phi\in\mathrm{Irr}(M_{0})
,
\widehat{\phi}\in\mathrm{Irr}(M^{\circ}_{\phi}\mid\phi)
and
E\leq{\underline{E}}(M^{\circ})_{\widehat{\phi}}
.
Let
E^{\prime}_{i}\leq\mathrm{Aut}(G_{i})
be the group of automorphisms induced by
E
. Assume that for each
i\in\{1,2\}
,
\phi_{i}\in\mathrm{Irr}(\left.\phi\right\rceil_{G_{i}})
extends to
M_{0}\rtimes E^{\prime}_{i}
.

(a)

Then
\phi
extends to
M_{0}E
.

(b)

If
v\in{{\mathbf{G}}}\setminus\{1_{{\mathbf{G}}}\}
(or equivalently
{\epsilon}_{1}={\epsilon}_{2}=-1
, see Table
5.1
),
2\mid f
and
h_{0}\in\ker(\phi)
, then
\phi
has an extension
\widetilde{\phi}
to
M_{0}E
with
vF_{q}\in\ker(\widetilde{\phi})
.

Proof.

Note first that
M_{0}E^{\prime}
is a subgroup of
(G_{1}E^{\prime}_{1})\times(G_{2}E^{\prime}_{2})
where
E^{\prime}\leq\mathrm{Aut}(M_{0})
is associated with
E
and hence

\phi
extends to
M_{0}E^{\prime}
.

Set
Z_{0}:=\operatorname{Z}(M_{0})
and
let
\{\eta\}=\mathrm{Irr}(\left.\phi\right\rceil_{Z_{0}})=\mathrm{Irr}(\left.\widehat{\phi}\right\rceil_{Z_{0}})
. As
\phi
is
E
-invariant and hence
E^{\prime}
-invariant,
\eta
is
E^{\prime}
-invariant as well.

Assume first that
\eta
extends to
Z_{0}E
.

We deduce that
\eta
extends to
Z_{0}E^{\prime}
, since
\eta
has degree
1
and
Z_{0}E^{\prime}=Z_{0}\rtimes E^{\prime}
, see
Proposition

2.2
(d).
We also observe

{\mathrm{C}}_{M_{0}{\underline{E}}(M^{\circ})}(M_{0})=\begin{cases}Z_{0}\left\langle vF_{q}\right\rangle&\text{if }({\epsilon}_{1},{\epsilon}_{2})=(-1,-1),\\
Z_{0}&\text{otherwise}\end{cases}

and hence
{\mathrm{C}}_{M_{0}E^{\prime}}(M_{0})=Z_{0}
, since
vF_{q}\in E
corresponds to the trivial element in
E^{\prime}
whenever
({\epsilon}_{1},{\epsilon}_{2})=(-1,-1)
. This leads to

(M_{0}E^{\prime},M_{0},\phi)\geq_{c}(Z_{0}E^{\prime},Z_{0},\eta),

by
Lemma

2.6
(a).
Theorem

2.8
then implies

\left(M_{0}E,M_{0},\phi\right)\geq_{c}\left(Z_{0}E,Z_{0},\eta\right).

Since
\eta
extends to
Z_{0}E
,
Lemma

2.6
(b) implies that
\phi
extends to
M_{0}E
. So we get (a) whenever
\eta
extends to
Z_{0}E
.

For part (b) we already note the following. If
\widetilde{\eta}
is an extension of
\eta
to
Z_{0}E
, then
Lemma

2.6
(b) again implies that some extension
\widetilde{\phi}
of
\phi
to
M_{0}E
satisfies

\displaystyle\mathrm{Irr}(\left.\widetilde{\phi}\right\rceil_{{\mathrm{C}}_{Z_{0}E}(M_{0})})

\displaystyle=\{\left.\widetilde{\eta}\right\rceil_{{\mathrm{C}}_{Z_{0}E}(M_{0})}\}.

(5.43)

Let us now check that
\eta
extends to
Z_{0}E
.

Assume first that
h_{0}\in\ker(\phi)
. Then
\left\langle h_{0}\right\rangle\leq\ker(\eta)
. According to
Lemma

5.13
and
Lemma

5.17
,
[E,E]\cap M^{\circ}\leq[{\underline{E}}(M^{\circ}),{\underline{E}}(M^{\circ})]\cap M^{\circ}\leq\left\langle h_{0}\right\rangle\leq\ker(\phi)
.
Proposition

2.2
(d) then implies that
\eta
extends to
Z_{0}E
.

We are left to consider the case where
h_{0}\notin\ker(\phi)
. Set
Z:=\left\langle h_{0},\mathbf{h}_{J_{1}}(\varpi),\mathbf{h}_{J_{2}}(\varpi)\right\rangle^{{{\nu F_{q}}}}
and
\widetilde{\eta}\in\mathrm{Irr}(\left.\widehat{\phi}\right\rceil_{\widetilde{Z}})
. We observe that
\eta
and
\widetilde{\eta}
are
E
-invariant. On the other hand, computations similar to
2.24
(b) show
[{\underline{E}}(M^{\circ}),Z]=\left\langle h_{0}\right\rangle
. As
\eta
is
E
-invariant and
h_{0}\notin\ker(\eta)
this implies
E\leq{\underline{E}}(M^{\circ})_{\widetilde{\eta}}\leq{\mathrm{C}}_{{\underline{E}}(M^{\circ})}(Z)
. According to
Lemma

5.13
and
Lemma

5.18
the Sylow
2
-subgroup of
{\mathrm{C}}_{{\underline{E}}(M^{\circ})}(Z)
and hence of
E
is abelian. This shows that
\eta
has an extension to the Sylow
2
-subgroup of
Z_{0}E
. Additionally observe that
E(M^{\circ})/Z_{0}
has a cyclic Hall
2^{\prime}
-subgroup and therefore
\eta
extends to
Z_{0}E
, see
[
Is
, Thm 11.32]
. Hence our claim in all cases and this finishes the proof of (a).

Next we ensure the statement in part (b). Here we assume
v\in{{\mathbf{G}}}\setminus\{1_{{\mathbf{G}}}\}
and
2\mid f
, hence
({\epsilon}_{1},{\epsilon}_{2})=(-1,-1)
. We observe that
vF_{q}\in{\mathrm{C}}_{{{{\mathbf{G}}}^{{{\nu F_{q}}}}}{\underline{E}}(M)}(M_{0})
. By assumption
h_{0}\in\ker(\phi)
and therefore
h_{0}\in\ker(\eta)
. Now
\eta
has an extension
\widetilde{\eta}
to
Z_{0}E
that can be assumed to have
vF_{q}
in its kernel since
\left\langle vF_{q}\right\rangle\cap Z_{0}\leq\left\langle h_{0}\right\rangle\leq\ker(\eta)
. According to (
5.43
), there exists an extension
\widetilde{\phi}
of
\phi
to
M_{0}E_{\phi}
with
vF_{q}\in\ker(\widetilde{\phi})
, as required in (b).
∎

5.D
Characters of
G_{1}
and
G_{2}

In order to prove
Theorem

5.20
we split
\mathrm{Irr}(M)
according to its constituents after restriction to
M_{0}=G_{1}.G_{2}
. In particular, we use the sets defined in

3.15
for the subgroups
G_{i}
of type D possibly of rank
\leq 3
in the sense of
Definition

3.17
.
Proposition

5.26
enumerates in a simplified fashion the cases that will be considered in the following sections.

Recall that the group
{\underline{E}}(M)
and the associated group
{\underline{E}}(M^{\circ})
from
Definition

5.10
stabilize
G_{1}
and
G_{2}
, see

(b) and

5.17
(d).

Recall
{\underline{E}}({\mathchoice{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{565795}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\displaystyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{565795}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\textstyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{439166}{204801}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptstyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{365185}{177495}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptscriptstyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}}^{\circ})={\underline{E}}(\mathchoice{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{565795}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\displaystyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{565795}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\textstyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{439166}{204801}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptstyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{365185}{177495}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptscriptstyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits})\left\langle n,h_{0}\right\rangle
and
{{{\mathchoice{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{412222}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\displaystyle G\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{412222}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\textstyle G\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{326422}{204801}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptstyle G\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{278933}{177495}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptscriptstyle G\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}}_{i}}}=\left\langle G_{i},\widehat{t}_{i},\widetilde{t}_{i}\right\rangle
.
The group
{\underline{E}}({\mathchoice{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{565795}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\displaystyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{565795}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\textstyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{439166}{204801}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptstyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{365185}{177495}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptscriptstyle M\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}}^{\circ})
also stabilizes
{\mathchoice{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{412222}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\displaystyle G\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{412222}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\textstyle G\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{326422}{204801}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptstyle G\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{278933}{177495}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptscriptstyle G\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}}_{1}
and
{\mathchoice{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{412222}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\displaystyle G\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{412222}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\textstyle G\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{326422}{204801}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptstyle G\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{278933}{177495}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptscriptstyle G\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}}_{2}
, see

Definition
\@upn
5.24

Let
i\in\{1,2\}
and write
{{{\underline{E}}_{M}({\mathchoice{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{412222}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\displaystyle G\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{412222}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\textstyle G\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{326422}{204801}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptstyle G\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{278933}{177495}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptscriptstyle G\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}}_{i})}}\leq\mathrm{Aut}({\mathchoice{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{412222}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\displaystyle G\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{412222}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\textstyle G\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{326422}{204801}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptstyle G\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{278933}{177495}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\scriptscriptstyle G\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}}_{i})
for the group of automorphisms of

}G_{i}{\underline{E}}_{M}(G_{i})_{\chi}\right.\right\}

and

\displaystyle{{\mathbb{D}_{i}}}

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\left\{\chi\,\left|({{\mathchoice{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{412222}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\displaystyle G\hss$\crcr}}}\limits}}_{i}}{{\underline{E}}}_{M}({\mathchoice{\mathop{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern 0.80002pt$\hss\resizebox{412222}{254863}{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{90.0}{(}}\hss$\crcr\cr$\hss\displaystyle
